# Commands · P · part 6

1,444 entries, pr-review to prior-attempts. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **pr-review**: Personal-overlay PR review of a foreign-repo PR — fetch diff + ticket, run code-smell heuristics, compute blast radius via CBM, check scope drift, dispatch cavecrew-reviewer, render terse findings locally. - **pr-review-loop**: Loop through GitHub PRs that need your review (new, or with new commits since your last review) — for each, pull the PR description plus every inline review thread and comment from gh, resolve the ticket from tickets/sprint.csv (asking the user only when it isn't there), check out the branch, and run /review-quick or the full multi-agent /review-pr ([eriks-briedis/claude-flowkit](https://github.com/eriks-briedis/claude-flowkit/blob/HEAD/commands/pr-review-loop.md))
- **pr-review-loop**: Confirm each iteration — review, fix, push same PR, re-review until 5/5 or cap 5 ([fb3rasp/agentic-workflow](https://github.com/fb3rasp/agentic-workflow/blob/HEAD/cursor/commands/pr-review-loop.md) · 3 copies)
- **pr-review-loop**: 1. Check PR for comments that are not resolved or addressed. 2. If there aren't any comments, sleep for 60 secs and check again. 3. Address comments and reply with comment tagging the original commenter to notify them of the update. 4. Resolve comment, if appropriate. 5. Repeat steps 1-4 until all comments are resolved and reviewers have no pending change requests or questions. ([mifkata/blog](https://github.com/mifkata/blog/blob/HEAD/public/archive/commands/pr-review-loop.md))
- **pr-review-merge**: Review PR before merging to check for best practices and potential issues ([matteocervelli/llms](https://github.com/matteocervelli/llms/blob/HEAD/.archive/claude-v1/commands/pr-review-merge.md) · ★25)
- **pr-review-parallel**: Review several open PRs at once. Each PR gets its own git worktree, so nothing touches your working tree and a dirty repo is fine. The parent does every gh/git call up front, then fans review agents out across the worktrees, then walks you through the findings one PR at a time. Display-only — it never posts to GitHub. - **primer-draft**: Drafter for the primer skill. EMPTY → DRAFTED transition. ([rjwalters/anvil](https://github.com/rjwalters/anvil/blob/HEAD/anvil/skills/primer/commands/primer-draft.md) · ★1)
- **primer-figures**: Figurer for the primer skill. Renders the teaching diagrams the drafter's figure plan references (mmdc → PNG) to exactly the exhibits/ paths the body points at, plus an optional PDF, reusing anvil/lib/render.py (pandoc-first, LaTeX opt-in) and anvil/lib/render_gate.py. No new rendering pipeline. Runs any time after draft/revise (no longer gated on AUDITED) so review + audit can score the rendered output. ([rjwalters/anvil](https://github.com/rjwalters/anvil/blob/HEAD/anvil/skills/primer/commands/primer-figures.md) · ★1)

Runs parallel with primer-audit. DRAFTED/REVISED → REVIEWED transition. ([rjwalters/anvil](https://github.com/rjwalters/anvil/blob/HEAD/anvil/skills/primer/commands/primer-review.md) · ★1)
- **primer-revise**: Reviser for the primer skill.
